輝達產品時程恐因領導層變動遞延，台股供應鏈重挫
2026 年 11 月 16 日
一、市場動態
1. 輝達執行長閃辭衝擊延燒，台股重挫逾 2500 點：黃仁勳於 11 月 12 日辭去輝達總裁、
執行長及董事職務，由財務長 Colette Kress 暫代執行長，資深董事 Harvey Jones 出
任首任董事長，市場擔憂產品時程與決策速度出現不確定，加上本周財報即將發布，
加深市場觀望情緒。台股今日受上週五美股半導體重挫拖累開低走低，收最低至
41,040 點、下跌 2635 點或 6.03%。台積電跌 135 元或 5.74%，聯發科跌逾 7%，台

達電、創意及台光電、欣興等 PCB／載板族群觸及跌停，高價 AI 股成為賣壓重心。
2. AI 晶片族群同步降溫，資金由「換股」轉為「降低整體曝險」：輝達換帥後，市場原預
期資金將轉進 AMD、博通及 ASIC 等替代方案，惟上週五美國半導體族群全面回檔，
替代受惠股跌幅甚至更大，顯示投資人擔憂的已非單一公司接班問題，而是 AI 投資循
環的整體風險。
二、下跌原因
1. 領導層更替落地，產品時程與決策速度出現不確定：輝達在此事件之前，約 60 名高階
主管直接向黃仁勳匯報，權力高度集中，接班空窗期決策速度恐下降。即使在其任內，
年更節奏已觸及供應鏈物理極限，Rubin CPX 已移出路線圖，Kyber 機櫃傳延至 2028
年。新任管理層若偏重成本與執行穩定，路線圖可能改為「大世代兩年、中間推出強
化版」，800V HVDC、共同封裝光學（CPO）、 M9 等級銅箔基板等升級導入時點恐再
遞延。
2. 規格升級放緩衝擊「本夢比」，高價零組件股首當其衝：台廠 2027 年成長有相當部分
來自 Vera Rubin 單櫃價值約為 GB300 兩倍，而單櫃價值主要來自規格升級。市場擔

憂高階液冷、HVDC 電源與光互連若由 2027 年遞延至 2028 年放量，供應鏈單價、
毛利率與 EPS 成長預估恐面臨下修；缺乏技術突破支撐的高本益比族群評價修正，今
日 PCB／CCL、散熱及電源族群跌停家數最多，即反映此一疑慮。
3. 生態系融資與出口管制成為新增變數：市場估計輝達每季自由現金流約 500 億至 600
億美元，若過渡期暫緩投資及融資交易，生態系資金供給可能暫時減少 500 億美元以
上；據報 Kress 曾反對為 OpenAI 提供最高 1,050 億美元擔保，新增融資審核恐趨嚴，
依賴輝達融資的 NeoCloud 及其供應商受影響較大。此外，事件涉及晶片流向中國之
指控，美方據報擬將訂定較嚴格出口限制，市場估潛在曝險約占輝達營收一成。
4. 期中選舉後，可能的出口管制收緊以及短線獲利了結需求：此事件涉及晶片流向中國之
指控，出口管制收緊將衝擊整體 AI 晶片；台股上週反彈近 4,000 點，短線獲利了結需
求升高，避險心態未退，放大 AI 權值股賣壓。

圖一：輝達執行長更替後，可能影響路徑分析
資料來源：元大銀行整理，Bloomberg，UBS
三、市場看法
1. 2027 年出貨大致鎖定，衝擊先落在評價而非營收：Vera Rubin 已於 8 月量產出貨、
2027 年放量；輝達對供應商之採購與產能承諾達 2,790 億美元，台積電、HBM 及主
要組裝廠 2027 年產能多已預訂。廣達法說表示迄今未見客戶調整訂單，鴻海亦預期
明年 AI 機櫃出貨高雙位數成長。依 ToAlpha 基本情境推算，輝達 CoWoS 份額相對
原本路徑 2027、2028 年僅分別減少 2 與 4 個百分點，CoWoS 總量僅減約 0.6%與

0.8%，影響主要在 2028 年後的產品節奏。
表一：輝達份額變動對 AI 平台與台股供應鏈之影響（情境推估）
項目（相對原本路徑） 2027 年 2028 年
輝達占 CoWoS 需求份額 −2 個百分點 −4 個百分點
輝達平台出貨量 −4.4% −8.9%
AMD 與 ASIC 平台出貨量 +2.5% +5.8%
CoWoS 總量（台積電、封測） −0.6% −0.8%
以輝達平台為主之台股 AI 營收 −3.4% −6.7%
GPU 與 ASIC 兩邊都有之台股 AI 營收 −1.3% −2.3%
以 ASIC 為主之台股 AI 營收 +1.5% +3.6%
資料來源：Bloomberg，元大銀行整理；此為 Bloomberg 情境推估之 AI 營收影響，非盈餘預估
2. 台股供應鏈由分化轉為齊跌，ASIC 族群抗跌性消失：依曝險分組，10 月 29 日至 11
月 12 日以輝達平台為主者平均下跌 14.0%，GPU 與 ASIC 兩邊都有者下跌 17.0%，以
ASIC 為主者下跌 9.4%，同期加權指數下跌 7.1%。兩邊都有之族群多為前波漲幅大的

規格升級零組件股，跌幅反而最深，顯示市場賣的是「評價」而非「訂單」；今日台股
盤勢延續上周五費半情緒，以 ASIC 為主及兩邊都有之代表股平均重挫逾 8%，以輝達
平台為主者跌約 4.4%，顯示賣壓轉向前波漲多、評價較高的 ASIC 與規格升級股，市
場已由「區分受益者」轉為「全面降低 AI 曝險」，短線錯殺機會浮現。

3. 算力多元化早於本次事件，Google TPU 與 AMD 最直接受惠：根據彭博在本次事件發
生前的統計，市場預估輝達占全球 CoWoS 需求比重將由 2026 年 56%降至 2027 年
45%，AMD 由 9%升至 20%，聯發科由 3%升至 7%，預計本次事件發生後，此趨勢
可能進一步加劇。由於 AMD、博通與雲端業者自研 ASIC 同樣高度依賴台積電先進製
程及台廠組裝與零組件，訂單多在台灣供應鏈內部移轉，台灣在 AMD、Google 供應
鏈同樣扮演重要角色。
表三：全球 CoWoS 需求之客戶比例
年度 CoWoS 需求（千片） 輝達 AMD 博通（Google TPU 等） 聯發科
2024 372 54% 11% 18% —
2025 689 62% 9% 12% —
2026（估） 1,394 56% 9% 22% 3%

2027（估） 2,694 45% 20% 18% 7%
資料來源：元大銀行整理，Bloomberg；2026、2027 年為估計值
4. 歷史經驗顯示 CEO 更替後股價無一致方向，基本面才是中期關鍵：回顧美股大型科技
公司交棒案例，計畫性交棒如 Apple、Microsoft 後 12 個月股價分別上漲 77.1%與
20.1%，但同為規劃性交棒的 Amazon 卻下跌 35.3%；突發撤換的 Intel 兩案，3 個月
內均下跌逾 9%，12 個月後則轉為正報酬。惟過去歷史經驗僅能作為參考，輝達後續
走勢仍取決於事件發展與基本面變化而定。

5. 本週三大事件為短線關鍵驗證點：（一）輝達將於美東 11 月 17 日（台灣時間 11 月 18
日凌晨）公布第三季財報，為首度由暫代執行長主持之法說會，焦點在 2028 會計年度
約七成成長指引是否重申、Vera Rubin 放量節奏及新增融資態度；（二）Anthropic 預
計 11 月 18 日定價上市，為檢驗 AI 市場熱度之指標；（三）黃仁勳預計感恩節前赴國
會作證，牽動晶片出口管制法案走向。若財報與指引穩健，市場有望由情緒面回歸基本

面。
四、投資建議
1. 科技股短期審慎、中期仍正向，AI 供應鏈基本面持續具支撐：黃仁勳辭任使輝達面臨
管理層接班、企業治理及政策調查等不確定性，加上 Fed 升息預期及 AI 資料中心監
管疑慮，市場短線波動可能延續。然而，該事件並未直接改變 AI 算力需求，且上週
CoreWeave Q3 財報營收年增 168%、台積電 10 月營收年增 59.7%，均顯示 AI 基礎
建設投資仍具動能。整體對科技股維持短期審慎、但中長期基本面仍在，台灣及日本
等具技術競爭優勢、獲利與現金流穩健的半導體及 AI 供應鏈亦值得留意。惟市場焦點
已由資本支出規模轉向投資報酬與現金流，後續仍須關注輝達財報及管理團隊能否維
持客戶信心。
2. 以股債平衡穿越波動，善用多元資產降低單一事件風險：台股權重高度集中於台積電
與 AI 供應鏈，對單一事件敏感度高。建議將集中度過高的台股科技部位，適度轉向全
球型或多重資產商品，搭配中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債穩定收益；待接班人
選、財報指引與出口管制方向明朗後，再逐步提高風險資產比重。

多重利空夾擊，全球 AI 股連日重挫
2026 年 11 月 20 日
一、市場動態
1. 美股 11/19 反彈遭逢賣壓：Anthropic 19 日於那斯達克掛牌，募資約 813 億美元創史
上最大 IPO，開盤大漲 34%、盤中市值一度突破 2 兆美元，終場漲幅收斂至 17.1%，
然激勵效果未能擴散至其他 AI 股，反被視為「利多出盡」的獲利了結訊號。美股四大
指數開高走低、全數重挫逾 2%：道瓊下跌 2.19%，為 6 月以來首度跌破 50,000 點；
標普 500 跌 2.02%、連六黑，逾 460 檔成分股收黑；那斯達克跌 2.25%、費半跌 2.41%，

VIX 盤中一度升近 29、創 3 月以來新高。個股方面，輝達早盤一度上漲 4.7%後翻黑，
收跌 3.3%，本月累計跌幅約 28%；AMD 遭放空機構 Hunterbrook 指控與受美國制裁
的中國伺服器業者往來，重挫 7.72%，高度依賴舉債擴張的 CoreWeave、Nebius 分
別重挫 6.1%與 9.4%。Fed 官員密集釋出鷹派訊號，警告 AI 建設熱潮恐推升通膨，市
場預估 12 月升息機率約 96%；初領失業金人數亦高於預期，增添景氣疑慮。
2. 亞股 11/20 續挫，台股同步承壓：亞股 11/20 早盤延續美股走勢，賣壓擴及亞洲 AI
硬體供應鏈，日經 225 與韓國 KOSPI 同步重挫逾 4%。台股 19 日下跌 1,768.02 點，
收 38,426.89 點，跌幅 4.40%，創史上收盤第 7 大跌點後，20 日再受美股下挫拖累，
加權指數持續走低，截至台灣時間下午 1 點 22 分為止，台股加權指數下挫 6.80%；台
積電、聯發科、台達電等權值股同步重挫。

表一：11 月以來台股主要重挫日一覽
日期 收盤 漲跌點數 漲跌幅 主要觸發因素

11/06 39,701.64 −1,873.08 −4.51% 選後資料中心監管疑慮、費半單日暴跌 12.25%

11/19 38,426.89 −1,768.02 −4.40% 輝達財報利多不漲、Fed 鷹派、跌破年線

資料來源：臺灣證券交易所、元大銀行整理；11/20 為盤中數據，台灣時間 11/20 上午 11:50
二、下跌原因
1. 美國對中 AI 管制全面升級，從「晶片」延伸到「算力」與「代工」：美國商務部工業暨
安全局（BIS）於 11 月 16 日發布臨時最終規則、並於 19 日生效，將管制由實體晶片

擴大至「提供雲端算力給中國客戶的第三國資料中心」，並將印尼、馬來西亞、新加坡、
泰國及巴西列為高轉運風險國家，同時限縮晶圓代工與封測廠的許可豁免。此次新規
由財政部與白宮國安會主導起草，反映黃仁勳辭任後政策方向明顯從嚴。對台股而言，

2. Fed 升息預期升溫，高利率壓縮成長股評價：Fed 10 月會議紀錄顯示，幾乎所有與會
者認為未來數月可能需要再升息，理事華勒更表示 12 月可考慮升息 2 碼；副主席傑佛
森與芝加哥 Fed 總裁古爾斯比亦示警 AI 建設熱潮正推升需求與通膨。依 CME 
FedWatch 約台灣時間上午 11 時的數據，市場預估 12 月升息 1 碼機率約 96%，美國
10 年期公債殖利率一度升至 5.12%。利率上升同時墊高資料中心融資成本、拉長投資

3. 輝達「利多不漲」且接班未定，市場由交易成長轉為交易風險：輝達 FY2027 第三季營
收與下季指引均優於預期，Vera Rubin 亦已量產出貨，但調整後毛利率指引下滑至 71%
～72%；董事會已表明黃仁勳不回任，並委託獵才公司物色新任執行長。財報利多仍無
法止跌，顯示市場對 AI 龍頭的定價重心，已由成長性轉向治理、政策與獲利率風險。

4. Anthropic 史上最大 IPO 引發資金排擠與「利多出盡」情緒：Anthropic 募資約 813
億美元，盤中市值一度突破 2 兆美元，但美股開高走低、晶片股由紅翻黑，市場將其視
為 AI 交易階段性高點的獲利了結訊號。市場亦解讀，大型 AI 模型公司直接上市，讓資
金可直接押注 AI 應用端，相對降低透過硬體供應鏈間接參與的需求，並在短期內吸納
部分 AI 配置資金。
5. 台股籌碼面賣壓與融資斷頭形成負向循環：外資 11 月累計賣超達 8,303 億元，台指期
淨空單維持約 9.9 萬口；台股跌破年線後融資維持率偏低，斷頭與停損賣壓同步出籠，
新台幣亦貶至 33 元附近，加重外資匯損考量。金管會雖自 20 日起實施限空令 2.0（個

股收跌逾 3.5%者隔日禁止平盤下放空、盤中借券賣出上限由 3%降至 1%），但短線仍
難完全抵銷被動賣壓。
資料來源：臺灣證券交易所、元大銀行整理
三、市場看法
1. AI 需求尚未反轉，但產業正由資本支出驅動轉向獲利驗證：我們認為，判斷 AI 產業是
否出現結構性反轉，關鍵應在於終端運算需求、CSP 資本支出及供應鏈訂單是否持續下

### Page 4

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
降，而非單純觀察股價跌幅。輝達第三季資料中心營收年增 110%，且 Vera Rubin 平
台已進入出貨階段，顯示先進運算設備需求仍具有實質支撐。此外，Reuters 引述 LSEG
資料指出，輝達財報公布後，分析師對下一財年調整後淨利的平均預估由 3,800 億美元
上修至 3,930 億美元，但股價評價反而轉趨保守。這項變化反映市場並未全面否定輝達
的獲利能力，而是開始要求更高的風險補償。我們認為，若未來 CSP 未大幅縮減資本
支出，AI 晶片與相關供應鏈仍具有中長期成長基礎；但若資本支出成長持續快於營收

及現金流改善，科技股評價仍可能承壓。
2. AI 信用風險仍較集中於高槓桿業者，尚未顯示全面性信用危機：目前市場對 AI 企業融
資風險的擔憂逐漸升高，但不同企業的財務承受能力存在明顯差異。大型 CSP 通常擁
有成熟的雲端、廣告或軟體業務，可透過營業現金流支應部分 AI 投資；相較之下，部
分新興資料中心業者高度依賴債務、設備融資或外部股權資金，對利率與資本市場環境
更加敏感。10 月 FOMC 會議紀錄指出，投資級公司債利差僅小幅擴大，且以歷史標準
衡量仍相對偏低；高收益債利差則擴大較明顯，尤其與資料中心融資有關的債券受到更
大衝擊。這顯示市場正在提高部分高風險企業的融資門檻，但尚不足以判定信用壓力已
全面擴散至整體投資級債市場。我們認為，後續仍應觀察相關企業的債務到期結構、再
融資需求與信用利差變化，判斷風險是否進一步擴大。
3. 科技股次產業將進一步分化：隨著市場由追逐 AI 投資規模轉向評估投資效益，科技次
產業未來表現可能明顯分歧。晶圓代工與先進封裝產業具有較高技術門檻，仍可受惠先

進運算晶片需求；HBM 及高階記憶體則具備 AI 伺服器成長題材，但其獲利仍需觀察供
需循環及價格變化；半導體設備業者則較容易受到客戶建廠時程及資本支出調整影響。
另一方面，部分尚未建立穩定營收模式的 AI 基礎建設業者，可能因融資成本與投資回
收壓力，面臨更大的評價修正。我們認為，科技股中期並非全面轉空，而是市場可能更
偏好具備技術競爭優勢、穩定訂單、獲利成長及自由現金流的企業。未來即使 AI 產業
整體維持成長，也不代表所有相關公司的營收、獲利及股價都能同步受惠。
4. Fed 政策仍有不確定性，但企業獲利成長可望部分抵銷評價壓力：目前 Fed 升息預期
已對全球科技股評價形成明顯壓力，但未來市場方向不僅取決於 12 月是否升息，更重
要的是升息幅度與後續政策路徑是否超出市場預期。然而，美國經濟與企業獲利目前尚
未出現全面性衰退跡象，若未來企業營收與獲利持續成長，即使本益比難以回到過去高
檔，仍有助於股市透過基本面改善逐步修復跌幅。我們認為，Fed 政策風險不容忽視，
但也不宜單憑升息預期就認定股市中長期多頭結構已全面改變。

5. 台股短線仍需消化籌碼壓力，但企業獲利成長仍可提供中期支撐：若以台股走勢來看，
其近期跌幅明顯超過部分國際市場，我們認為除受到美股科技股修正影響，也與大型電
子權值股集中、外資調節及短線市場流動性下降有關。不過，根據金管會公布數據，截
至 2026 年 10 月底，國內上市櫃公司累計營收約 56.75 兆元，年增 39.05%；前三季
累計稅前淨利約 6.61 兆元，年增 86.43%，顯示整體企業獲利仍具成長支撐。此外，
金管會自 11 月 20 日起擴大限空措施，對收盤跌幅逾 3.5%的個股實施次日平盤下放空
限制，並將盤中借券賣出數量上限由 3%降至 1%。我們認為，政策措施有助降低部分

### Page 5

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
短線賣壓，但市場真正止穩仍需觀察外資流向、融資去槓桿是否趨緩，以及主要電子權
值股的股價與基本面是否恢復穩定。
6. 後續觀察重點：（1）美國下週公布核心 PCE 物價指數，市場預期年增 3.2%，將影響
12 月升息幅度；（2）12 月 FOMC 升息 1 碼或 2 碼，以及 2027 年升息路徑；（3）輝
達新任執行長人選與黃仁勳赴參議院作證情況，牽動出口管制後續走向；（4）BIS 新規
執行細節及東南亞資料中心許可審查進度。
四、 投資建議
1. 短線嚴控槓桿與部位集中度，選股邏輯轉向「現金流與訂單能見度」優先：我們認為，

AI 產業成長動能尚未確認反轉，但市場已不再接受單純依靠資本支出擴張支撐評價。
前述利空因素短期內恐難完全消除或明朗，市場短線仍將隨著消息面的發展而變化。我
們預期後續投資人將更重視科技股的訂單能見度、企業獲利、資產負債表及自由現金
流。同時，台灣在先進製程、先進封裝及 AI 伺服器領域仍具競爭優勢，中期基本面展
望維持偏正向。然而，近期指數連續重挫，反映市場流動性與籌碼壓力尚未解除，短期
仍須觀察外資賣壓、融資水位及美股科技股走勢。投資人宜採取分批及分散方式參與中
長期產業成長，同時避免過度集中於融資需求高、獲利能見度較低的 AI 企業。
2. 以股債平衡穿越波動，固定收益「先短後長」：Fed 仍處升息循環，長天期債券價格仍
有波動風險，但美國公債目前具有高殖利率吸引力與防禦價值，建議以中短天期美國公
債、高信評投資等級債及美元貨幣型商品鎖定相對高檔的收益，待升息循環接近尾聲再
逐步拉長存續期間。投資級債則受惠相對穩健的信用品質，惟仍須關注 AI 相關企業信
用風險是否進一步擴散。

強茂(2481 TT) Buy

車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器拉貨帶動，投資建議 Buy。  
投資建議：展望未來，隨著車用市佔率持續擴大，加上雲端資
料中心與 AI 伺服器新世代平台功率元件拉貨需求攀升，給予 
Buy 的投資建議，目標價 160 元。 
車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器拉貨，3Q26 獲利三率三升：  
強茂 3Q26 營收 43.50 億元（QoQ +10.0%、 YoY +33.0%），主
因車用及 AI 需求帶動功率元件需求轉強；毛利率因產品組合
優化提升至 37.3%（ QoQ +4.1 ppts、 YoY +6.0 ppts）；營業利益 
6.97 億元（QoQ +50.1%、 YoY +152.3%）；稅後淨利 6.49 億
元（QoQ +33.0%、 YoY +114.0%），3Q26 EPS 為 1.70 元。 
產品組合車用比重提升，產能佈局強化東南亞供應： 
YTD 3Q26 終端應用營收比重以車用 35%最高（YOY+2ppts），
車用產品營收較去年同期大幅成長 47.2%。AI 與雲端運算營收
佔整體營收逾 12%，拉貨將逐步提升。集團擁有三座晶圓廠及
五座後段封測廠，隨著 2026 年下半年越南胡志明市新封測廠加
入營運，進一步強化東南亞供應能力。預估 2026 年營收 
160.48 億元（YoY +22.6%），EPS 為 5.47 元。 
車用市佔率持續擴大，MOSFET 業務加速放量與定價能力提
升：隨著 AI 伺服器機櫃功耗提高，強茂成功切入美系 CSP 的  
ASIC 伺服器與 AI 機櫃電源供應鏈，主攻 Hot-Swap（熱插拔）
與 ORing（冗餘電源管理兩大關鍵應用），推動 AI 佔比朝 15%
以上邁進。車用方面，除了 40V 車規中低壓 MOSFET 持續放
量外，更推出碳化矽（SiC）MOSFET 搶攻日歐美市場。綜上所
述，預估強茂 2027 年營收 192.31 億元（YoY +19.8%），EPS 
8.41 元。基於車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器新世代平台拉貨需求攀
升，給予 Buy 投資建議，目標價 160 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
在  ESG 永續發展表現上，強茂積極落實環境保護、供應鏈管理與綠色技術
創新三大面向： 
環境永續：持續投入節能減碳與能源轉型，透過製程節能改善及再生能源建
置，2025 年全年節電約 91.1 萬度，減少約 432 公噸 CO₂e 排放；太陽能系
統全年發電量突破 263 萬度，穩健提升綠電使用比重並朝向低碳營運邁進。
責任供應鏈：依循責任商業聯盟(RBA)標準深化供應鏈管理，年度完成 75 家
供應商 RBA 稽核且合格率達 100%，攜手合作夥伴打造具韌性的永續價值
鏈。 
創新與產品發展：持續深耕高效率功率半導體研發，2025 年集團累計有效
專利達 102 件，並維持 90 分以上的客戶滿意度，以高效能半導體解決方案
協助終端客戶提升能源使用效率，共同響應全球淨零碳排趨勢。

公司簡介： 
強茂成立於 1986 年，為台灣功率半導體領導廠商之一，主要從事離散元件
（ Discrete）之研發、製造與銷售，產品涵蓋 MOSFET、Schottky、Rectifier、
TVS、 Zener、 Bridge Transistor、 ESD Protection 及  SiC 等。公司母公司專
注於核心元件開發，並透過關係企業與子公司進行垂直與水平整合，包含熒
茂光學、凡甲科技、虹冠電子、汎星半導體及新貿科技。強茂在低功率矽整
流器領域位居全球第四大供應商，並持續聚焦車用電子、雲端資料中心與 AI
伺服器等高附加價值應用。 
主要競爭對手分析 
1. 國際一線 IDM 大廠（高階車用與 AI 伺服器主要對手） 
強茂近年積極搶攻歐美車廠與 AI 機櫃市佔，主要在以下領域與國際巨頭
正面交鋒（並受惠於供應鏈去中化或對手產能受限之轉單效應）： 
• 安世半導體（Nexperia）：最直接的車用與小訊號元件競爭對手。安
世在車用小訊號二極體、ESD/TVS 保護元件及中低壓 MOSFET 市
佔率極高；每當安世出現產能吃緊或地緣政治/出口管制干擾時，歐美
Tier 1 車廠往往優先將訂單轉移至強茂。 
• 意法半導體（STMicroelectronics） 、 安森美（onsemi） 、 英飛凌
（ Infineon） ：全球車用與工業功率半導體前三大巨頭，在車規
MOSFET、SiC（碳化矽）及 AI 伺服器電源管理擁有完整生態系。強
茂以高性價比及交期彈性切入其二供（Second Source）體系。 
• 威世（Vishay）、達爾（Diodes Incorporated）、羅姆（ROHM）：
在整流二極體、蕭特基二極體及中低壓 MOSFET 產品線高度重疊，
是強茂在美系與日系客戶端的主要比價與份額競爭者。 
2. 台灣分離式元件同業 
• 台半（5425）：在整流二極體與車用 MOSFET 布局深厚（切入 Tier

4 群益投顧 
帶動功率元件需求轉強；毛利率因產品組合優化提升至 37.3% ，
QoQ+4.1ppts，YoY+6.0ppts；營業利益 6.97 億元，QoQ+50.1%，YoY+152.3%，
呈現大幅提升；稅後淨利 6.49 億元，QoQ+32.96%， YoY+113.95%， 3Q26 
EPS 1.70 元，3Q26 財報表現亮眼。 
產品組合車用比重提升，產能佈局強化東南亞供應： 
YTD 3Q26 終端應用營收比重以車用 35%最高，年增 2 ppts，車用產品營收
較去年同期大幅成長 47.2%。 AI 與雲端運算營收佔整體營收逾 12%，受惠
新世代平台規格推進拉貨穩健。集團擁有三座晶圓廠及五座後段封測廠，隨
2026 年下半年越南胡志明市新封測廠加入營運，進一步強化東南亞第三供
應能力，2H26 營運持續增長，4Q26 營運預估與 3Q26 持平，預估 2026 年
營收 160.48 億元(YOY+22.56%)， EPS 為 5.47 元。 
車用市佔率持續擴大，MOSFET 業務加速放量與定價能力提升： 
強茂本身具有歐美車廠供應鏈能力，在安世半導體產能與出貨受阻後，客戶
端為確保供貨安全，加速將小訊號二極體與保護元件（ESD/TVS）訂單轉移
至強茂，使推升其車用營收比重與整體市佔率。隨著 AI 伺服器機櫃功耗
大幅提高，資料中心對供電穩定度與電源轉換效率的要求越發嚴苛。在成功
切入美系 CSP（雲端服務大廠）的 ASIC 伺服器與 AI 機櫃電源供應鏈後，
強茂主攻 Hot-Swap（熱插拔）與 ORing（冗餘電源管理）兩大關鍵應用。這
類高電流供電路徑需要具備「超低導通電阻（Low RDS(on)）」的中高壓
MOSFET 來降低功耗與發熱，其 ASP 遠高於傳統消費性元件，預估 AI 佔比
可朝 15%以上邁進。 
車用展望方面，目前佔強茂整體營收比重約 35%。隨著電動車與智慧座艙滲
透率提升，車內馬達驅動、電池管理系統（BMS）及車載充電器對 MOSFET 
的用量呈倍數增長。強茂除了既有的 40V 車規中低壓 MOSFET 持續放量
外，更進一步推出碳化矽（SiC）MOSFET 搶攻日本與歐美車用市場，產品
線從小訊號保護元件成功延伸至高毛利的動力與電源核心。綜上所述，預估
強茂 2027 年營收 192.31 億元(YOY+19.8%)， EPS 8.41 元。 
投資建議：展望未來，隨著車用市佔率持續擴大，加上雲端資料中心與 AI 
伺服器新世代平台功率元件拉貨需求攀升，給予 Buy 的投資建議，目標價
160 元。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

MPI hosted its 3Q26 earnings call on November 16. Key takeaways:
There could be further upside in 2027/28 capacity expansion: The company
reiterated that it would add at least 2.5mn pins for MEMS and 0.5mn pins for VPC in
2027. It may raise the numbers after its budget plan is finalized in December. MPI
expects to maintain aggressive capacity expansion in 2028, given strong demand.

2027 guidance appears conservative: The company guided for 2027 revenue to be
up at least 50% YoY with gross margin >60% –   conservative vs. consensus
expectations of 2027 revenue doubling. However, we expect MPI to beat its own
guidance  and raise it gradually, per previous practice of amendment.
In qualification for second insertion 2 customer: The company is  still in
qualification for insertion 2. However, it confirmed it has a second significant

customer; the schedule of the qualification could be similar to the first one. MPI said
it would be the only supplier for the first  customer's  insertion 2 process. CPO could
make up a double-digit share of 2028 total revenue.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Company feedback suggests the fundamental outlook for 2027
could be better than feared, especially for top-tier companies in
the AI hardware supply chain. Consumer electronics companies
are passing through the worst of the downturn, with a potential
recovery likely in 2027.

Greater China Technology Hardware

Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Recent Research:                  Global Technology and
Telecom: 2026 AlphaWise Smartphone Survey:
Another Down Year That's Not Yet Priced In (16
Nov 2026)
While we believe some investors are turning more bearish on AI amid top-down
uncertainty, bottom-up discussions with supply chain companies suggest the
outlook for 2027 is better than feared. Within the AI universe, our key
recommendations include: Innolight, Largan, Wistron, Hon Hai, Wiwynn, Unimicron,

EMC, Lenovo, Delta, Bizlink and Huaqin.
For non-AI-driven consumer electronics companies, we believe any slower memory
price increases could provide a positive catalyst. Companies under our coverage
supplying components to Apple appear better positioned to deliver growth in 2027,
in our view, while some Android suppliers we spoke with anticipate potentially
weaker volumes. Our key recommendations include Xiaomi, Luxshare-H, Crystal and
Transsion.

AI servers, optical interconnects and infrastructure
- Hon Hai – AI demand remains strong; additional rack assembly share expected.
Management reiterated 30%+ AI demand growth in 2027, with no recent
deterioration. ASICs should remain approximately 20% of cloud revenue, as higher
VR200 rack prices offset faster ASIC growth. Hon Hai expects to gain rack assembly
share through the addition of one hyperscaler and one neocloud customer.
- Zhongji Innolight – Most 2027 contracts have confirmed volumes and prices, with

some customers providing 2028 guidance. Customers have increased 1.6T orders,
partly offset by lower 800G orders, with no backlog cancellations. NPO demand
has strengthened; volume production is scheduled for 2H27, with stronger growth in
2028. Annual transceiver price reductions remain normal, but silicon photonics,
product upgrades and a more efficient one-laser-to-four-lanes solution should
support margins.
Continued on the next page.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Innolight, Largan, Wistron, Hon Hai, Wiwynn, Unimicron, Lenovo, Delta, Bizlink
and Huaqin are our preferred stocks for the AI theme.
We believe Xiaomi, Luxshare-H, Crystal and Transsion are likely to enjoy a
gradual recovery over the following quarters.
November 22, 2026 11:03 PM GMT

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Summary of Our Summit Feedback and Links to Individual
Company Reports
- Quanta – Improving AI product mix supports profitability; notebooks remain weak. Non-
NVIDIA projects should contribute a double-digit percentage of AI server revenue in 2027,
up from single digits in 2026, and generally carry higher margins. Quanta is negotiating
additional GPU consignment arrangements, although most remain unfinalized. Neocloud

exposure is limited to a single-digit percentage of total revenue.
- Huaqin – China SuperPoD deployments offer a multi-year growth opportunity. Volume
deliveries to two major Chinese cloud service providers began in 2H26 and should expand
to three in 2027. Huaqin has completed development of a 3.2T NPO system solution, with
a 6.4T solution under development, and plans to begin customer promotion in 2027.
- Bizlink – The company believes >20% sequential revenue growth in 4Q26 is achievable,

supported by organic growth and Interplex. Power interconnect dollar content could
double on the next GPU platform, alongside market share gains; separate power racks
offer further potential depending on customer deployment. Optical diversification
includes fiber patch cords, active optical cables from 2H27, and CPO initiatives.
- Delta Electronics – Management sees potential for double-digit sequential revenue
growth in 4Q26 and stronger-than-seasonal performance in 1Q27. Component tightness is

likely to ease from 2Q27. High-voltage direct-current power rack shipments should start in
4Q26, initially with limited revenue contribution. Delta cited 80VDC as the current
reference design for the 2H27 GPU platform, although the design remains unfinalized.
- Lite-On – Management expects revenue growth of 5–10% QoQ in 4Q26, followed by
another single-digit increase in 1Q27, and reiterated its 70% cloud revenue growth target
for 2027. Power supplies, battery backup units and power racks should benefit from rising

AI cluster power consumption. Normalized gross/operating margin targets are 25–
30%/13–15%. Shipments of 800VDC power racks should begin in 3Q27.
Non-AI-driven consumer electronics
- Luxshare – Consumer electronics share gains, new AI devices and PC ODM development
offer growth opportunities. Luxshare also targets large-scale optical transceiver
production in 2027, complementing its electrical interconnect, liquid cooling and AI power
offerings.
- Sunny Optical – Sunny aims to sustain handset lens revenue despite industry weakness

and gain module share at Samsung and Huawei. Potential new product launches for a key
customer could support strong XR growth in 2027, while robotics offers a multi-year
optical demand opportunity. Passive optical interconnect components are undergoing
customer qualification.
- Transsion – 3Q26 is likely to mark the earnings trough; management targets positive
revenue and earnings growth in 2027. Memory inventories are normalizing, and
management considers further substantial memory price increases less likely. Higher

processor costs should have a relatively limited impact on the overall bill of materials.
Management targets energy storage revenue of Rmb1.5–2.0 billion in 2026 and more than

研 究 報 告                
旺矽(6223.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
探針卡需求強勁挹注短期營運動能強勁、CPO 設備進度如期  
 投資評價與建議 
目前探針卡廠商評價普遍修正至20~35倍，尤其以短期營運相對穩健之廠商
(如精測、旺矽)仍相對有撐，而我們觀察公司探針卡需求強勁支撐短期營運動
能強勁，並重申CPO設備驗證進度如期、客戶數持續增加，故仍看好評價仍有
望回到合理區間之中上緣。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26營收有望雙位數季增：公司預估4Q26營收有望季增5~10%、毛利率
維持近3Q26之高檔水準，觀察探針卡BB Ratio均維持高檔水準，且擴產
計畫不變。基於公司10月營收達22.3億元創下新高、財測⼀向較為保守，
預估營收有望季增12.7%至66.6億元，毛利率61.8%，稅後EPS 21.82元。 
積極擴增MEMS產能、重申CPO客戶需求與進度如期：展望2027年：
(1)VPC和MEMS明年底有望擴至250萬針、600萬針，擴充幅度仍具有上
修空間，當前VPC主要客戶未有升級至MEMS規劃，至於MEMS主要貢獻
則來自於美系客戶伺服器CPU；(2)insertion 2客戶將於12月通知驗證結
果，1H27有望獲得量產機訂單，至於insertion 3已如期出貨工程機給3間
客戶，並預期1H27將陸續獲得量產機訂單，惟公司預估CPO設備營收顯
著貢獻時間點將落在2028年，故我們同步調整營收、獲利。 
 投資風險 
CPO進展不如預期、探針卡擴產針數下修。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況 
3Q26獲利優於預期：3Q26營收季增12.8%至59億元，連續七季創下歷史新高，毛利率61.7%，稅後EPS 19.18元，
其中營收、獲利均優於預期，推估主因為探針卡產品組合改善，加上於2Q26進行之探針卡報價調整完整貢獻3Q
營收(包含CPC漲價20%、取消VPC和MEMS 10%價格優惠)，另公司營業費用管控得宜也同步挹注獲利。 
估4Q26營收有望雙位數季增：公司預估4Q26營收有望季增5~10%、毛利率有望維持近3Q26之高檔水準，我們
推估動能包含：(1)維持VPC、MEMS月產能今年底分別擴至200萬針、350萬針之進展；(2)訂單出貨則觀察探針卡
BB Ratio均維持高檔水準，其中CPC今年前10個月維持滿載、目前BB Ratio接近1，至於MEMS、VPC則超過1，
考量公司探針卡交期已明顯延⻑至4~6個月，隱含本季探針卡營收仍有望持續提升。基於公司10月營收達22.3億
元創下新高、財測⼀向較為保守，預估本季營收有望季增12.7%至66.6億元，毛利率61.8%，稅後EPS 21.82元。 
積極擴增MEMS產能、重申CPO客戶需求與進度如期：展望2027年，我們觀察：(1)探針卡中，明年CPC月產能維
持50萬針、VPC和MEMS明年底則有望擴至250萬針、600萬針，產能擴充幅度具有上修空間，且強調2028年維持
積極擴產策略，訂單貢獻中，VPC主要客戶近年未有升級至MEMS探針卡之規劃，預期該客戶AI ASIC訂單將支撐
明年VPC需求，至於MEMS明年主要貢獻則來自於美系客戶伺服器CPU，且公司持續爭取潛在客戶AI ASIC訂單，
另規劃於2H27量產之PCB廠有望挹注探針卡成本優化；(2)CPO設備中，insertion 2如我們先前所提，於3Q26底
～4Q26初送樣至客戶端進行驗證，公司預估客戶將於12月通知驗證結果，根據目前客戶回饋正向，預期1H27有
望獲得量產機訂單，此外近期有另⼀客戶與公司接洽中(公司預估1H27獲得驗證結果)，至於insertion 3則已如期
出貨工程機給3間客戶，也將陸續交機給另3間客戶，預計1H27將陸續獲得insertion 3量產機訂單，惟考量接單至
出貨、裝機驗收之時間較⻑，公司認為CPO相關設備顯著營收貢獻時間點將落在2028年，我們則同步下修明年相
關設備之營收認列數量。綜上所述，公司預估明年營收有望年增至少50%、毛利率維持在60%以上；我們則預估
明年營收有望年增58.6%至344.6億元，較先前下修主要反映部分CPO設備遞延至2028年認列營收，毛利率63.2%，
稅後EPS 117.77元。

精測(6510 TT) Buy

精測受惠 HPC 測試需求，2027 年 營 收、獲 利 皆 可 望 再 創 新
高 ， 給 Buy 的 投 資 評 等 。  
投 資 建 議：精 測 受 惠 於 HPC(TPU、AI5 等 )測 試 需 求，2027 年營
收 、 獲 利 皆 可 望 再 創 新 高 ，給 Buy 的 投 資 評 等 。  
精測 3Q26 營 收 、 獲 利 皆 創 單 季 新 高 ： 精測 3Q26 營收 19.05 億
元，QoQ+16.17%\YoY+53.35%；毛 利 率 57.26%、營 益 率 35.21%；
稅後純益 5.51 億 元，QoQ+11.61%\YoY+100.03%；稅 後 EPS 16.76
元。3Q26 營 收 、 獲 利 皆 創 單 季 新 高 ， 雖 然 因 新 產 品 開 始 認 列 營
收 ， 毛 利 率 略 為 季 減 ， 但 獲 利 成 長 還 是 大 於 營 收 成 長 。  
精 測 有 TPU 與 AI5 商機： 精 測 表 示 ， 美 系 客 戶 的 TPU 供 應 商
維 持 既 有 架 構 ， 預 期 在  TPU8 專 案 中 將 扮 演 一 定 角 色 ， 不 過 目
前 尚 未 正 式 發 包，相 關 專 案 預 計 於 2H26 啟 動。此 外，針 對 另 一
客戶的 AI5 專 案， 依 客 戶 規 劃 時 程， 預 計 06/2026 進 行 工 程 卡
交 付 ， 並 預 計 於 2026 年底至 2027 年 初 進 入 量 產 。  
精 測 新 產 能 持 續 開 出：2026 年 營 運 逐 季 成 長 趨 勢 明 確，3Q26 營
收 有 機 會 雙 位 數 季 增 ， 08/2026 、 03/2027 會 有 比 較 大 的 產 能 開
出 。 目 前 交 期 約 5~8 周 ， 新 產 能 客 戶 驗 證 時 間 約 需 一 季 ， 2027
年 營 運 健 康 成 長 。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 24 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
在 環 境 保 護 方 面 ， 中 華 精 測 於 2022 年 開 始 自 主 性 導 入 溫 室 氣 體 盤 查 作 業 ，
除 了 計 算 碳 使 用 總 量 外，也 了 解 如 何 節 能 及 尋 找 替 代 能 源，並 推 動 各 項 環 保
節 能 減 碳 措 施、減 少 溫 室 氣 體 排 放 量 等，以 期 減 緩 與 調 適 氣 候 變 遷 所 帶 來 之
營 運 衝 擊 。  
在 社 會 責 任 方 面 ， 中 華 精 測 遵 守 相 關 法 規 及 國 際 人 權 公 約 ， 持 續 營 造 友 善 、
平 等、自 由 的 工 作 環 境，對 於 提 升 員 工 福 祉 不 遺 餘 力，且 不 定 期 舉 辦 家 庭 活
動 及 獎 勵 同 仁 結 婚 、 生 育 、 旅 遊 津 貼 等 員 工 福 利 。  
在 公 司 治 理 營 運 績 效 方 面 ， 中 華 精 測 連 續 兩 年 於 公 司 治 理 評 鑑 排 名 前 5%，
並於 2022 年 榮 獲 上 櫃 公 司 治 理 評 鑑  Top5%之 佳 績，秉 持 誠 信 正 直 原 則，定
期 宣 導 及 教 育 訓 練 內 線 交 易、誠 信 廉 潔 等 法 律 知 識，確 保 公 司 員 工 遵 守 誠 信
經 營 守 則 之 落 實 。  
公 司 簡 介 ：  
中 華 精 測 科 技 股 份 有 限 公 司 (6510 TT)設 立 於 08/2005，總 部 及 生 產 工 廠 皆 位
於 桃 園 平 鎮，為 中 華 電 信 所 屬 關 係 企 業，前 身 為 中 華 電 信 研 究 所 內 部 之 高 速
PCB 團 隊 ，2001 年 起 開 始 嘗 試 研 發 設 計 測 試 載 板 (Load board) 及 探 針 卡 載 板
(Probe PCB) 。  
中 華 精 測 主 要 從 事 晶 圓 測 試 板 、 IC 測 試 板 、 技 術 服 務 與 其 他 等 業 務 ， 提 供
半導體產業測試所需的介面板服務，用於晶圓測試之探針卡專用印刷電路
板 (Probe PCB) ， 及 用 於 IC 測 試 之 載 版 (Load Board) 。  
中 華 電 信 (2412 TT) 持股中 華 投 資 公 司 約 89.0% ； 中 華 投 資 持 股 精 測 約
33.6%；聯發科(2454 TT)於 2015 年，透 過 旗 下 翔 發 投 資 公 司 入 股 中 華 精 測，
目 前 持 股 約 0.5%。  
精 測 的 台 灣 同 業 包 含 旺 矽 (6223 TT)、穎崴(6515 TT)、 雍 智 科 技 (6683 TT)…
等。精 測 的 客 戶 包 含 美 系 HPC 公 司、台 系 IC 設 計 公 司、美 系 IC 設 計 公 司，
台 系 晶 圓 代 工 廠 …等。 
營 收 占 比 _產 品 別 ：  
產 品 目 前 毛 利 率 排 序 ： 整 組 探 針 卡 >FT 用的 loadboard>SLT 板 / 老化板。 
晶 圓 測 試 卡 包 含 PCB 與 整 組 探 針 卡 。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 24 日

3 群益投顧 
資 料 來 源 ： 精 測 法 說 會 簡 報 。  
營 收 占 比 _產 品 應 用 別 ：

資 料 來 源 ： 精 測 法 說 會 簡 報 。  
精測 4Q26 毛 利 率 可 能 季 減 ：  
精 測 之 前 說 3Q26 毛 利 率 區 間 為 50~55%，但 實 際 毛 利 率 優 於 預 期，因 09/2026
才 開 始 出 毛 利 率 較 差 的 老 化 板，所 以 對 3Q26 毛 利 率 影 響 有 限，精 測 估 4Q26
毛利率 50~55%， 但 還 是 要 看 產 品 組 合 。  
精 測 估 2027 年 營 收 、 獲 利 雙 位 數 成 長 ：  
精測估 1Q27 營 收 將 介 於 3Q26~4Q26 間，精 測 表 示 以 目 前 訂 單 的 狀 況，2027
年 營 收 維 持 50%的 成 長 ， EPS 的 成 長 也 是 可 以 期 待 。  
整 組 探 針 卡 於 往 年 的 合 併 營 收 占 比 約 20~30%，精 測 2027 年 挑 戰 占 比 30%，
但 也 要 看 其 他 產 品 的 成 長 性 。  
現 在 由 於 營 收 規 模 成 長，精 測 建 議 費 用 率 預 估 為 20~25%，用 於 研 發、留 才、
與 國 際 大 廠 的 合 作 等 。  
TPU 工 程 驗 證 產 品 預 計 11/2026 開 始 出 貨 ：  
傳聞 TPU 產 品 因 封 裝 問 題 ， 量 產 時 間 遞 延 ， 精 測 表 示 工 程 驗 證 產 品 的 出 貨
沒 有 遞 延 ， 量 產 時 間 預 計 會 是 1H27， 量 產 的 數 量 還 沒 有 確 定 。  
TPU 專 案，精 測 將 於 11/2026 開 始 出 貨 工 程 驗 證 用 的 整 組 探 針 卡，之 後 量 產
的 數 量 還 沒 確 定 。 記 憶 體 用 的 整 組 探 針 卡 也 會 於 2026 年 送 樣 。  
美 系 車 用 晶 片 大 廠 的 訂 單 ， 精 測 也 於 2026 年 開 始 出 貨 工 程 驗 證 的 產 品 ， 預
計將於 2027 年 量 產 ， 之 後 量 產 的 數 量 還 沒 確 定 。 工 程 驗 證 結 束 ， 到 正 式 量
產 通 常 需 要 1~2 季。 
除了 TPU 專 案 以 外 ， 精 測 也 積 極 驗 證 北 美 、 韓 國 客 戶 ， 精 測 估 2027 年 的 產
能 與 稼 動 率 皆 能 維 持 高 檔 。  
精 測 有 發 CB， 三 廠 產 能 預估 4Q28 開出：  
精測 07/29 重 訊 決 議 發 行 CB， 預 計 募 資 20 億 元 ， 主 要 用 於 新 廠 的 工 程 款 ，

8 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】  
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 ≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <15%

中立(Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等  
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整  
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】  
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為 四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等 係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。   
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」 及 「 區 間 操 作 」 均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。  
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ：  
1. 達 目 標 價 。  
2. 雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。  
3. 推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】 
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

梁會鑫 C00255@ibfs.com.tw 
7722.TT LINEPAY 
 累計營收再度創高，惟匯損衝擊獲利增幅收窄

[調升評價]   
   公司簡介 
公司於 2015 年設立，為國內第三方支付龍頭，丁雄注為董事長兼總經理，分別有台
北富邦銀行、聯邦銀行入股，並持有兩間子公司，於 2025 年 7 月經金管會核准設立
電子支付子公司後，已於 2025 年 12 月啟用電子支付。   
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 303 
【前次投資建議】區間操作，303 
【大盤指數/股價】36033 / 225

➢ 前 9 月營收雙位數增長，惟毛利率因營業費用轉列營業成本縮減 
LinePay 累計前 9 月營收為 64.33 億元，YoY+15%，而 LinePay 前 9 月
毛利率為 24.9%，YoY-8.6ppts.，主因電子支付上線後，原本列為營業
費用的管理與研發支出，部分轉列至營業成本所致，惟毛利率亦較 1H26
有所提升，反映電子支付營收缺口逐漸補上，而營業淨利率仍受成本及
費用增長而降至 6.8%，YoY-1.9ppts.，且 3Q26 稅後淨利受業外匯兌損
失影響下較 3Q25 YoY-22.6%下，令整體 2026 年前 9 月稅後淨利僅增
至 4.35 億元，YoY+5.7%， EPS 為 6.12 元。 
➢ 2026 年營收預估將因短期營收出現缺口使年增低於過往水準 
綜觀 4Q26，LinePay 10 月營收為 7.33 億元，YoY+4.1%，占 4Q26 達
成率為 31.6%，營收年增低於過去水準反映短期營收出現缺口所致，考
量前 9 月核心手續費年增維持逾 20%，此外 4Q26 亦有百貨週年慶、電
商節慶與旅遊旺季可望為支付交易量帶來季節性動能，綜合考量之下，
投研部預估 4Q26 營收為 23.17 億元，QoQ+5%，YoY+6.4%，稅後淨
利則為 1.56 億元，QoQ+11.8%，YoY+63.3%，EPS 2.29 元。 
➢ 電支業務有望加速拓展，調升至買進評等 
展望 2026 年，考量 LinePay 與眾多銀行推出多檔活動，預期將可挹注
全年交易量提升，並帶動營收成長，並參考 LinePay 開始施行電子支付
業務，因而投研部預估 2026 年營收為 87.5 億元，YoY+11.2%，此外，
因電子支付業務毛利率較現行手續費毛利率高，預估隨電子支付業務 4
月轉虧為盈後，將有望帶動毛利率逐季改善，因而預估將使毛利率較
2026 年前 9 月增長至 25.2%，YoY-7.5ppts.，預期亦能使淨利有所增
長，稅後淨利則為 5.91 億元，YoY+16.5%，EPS 為 8.58 元。 
綜合前述，以 11/20 收盤價 225 元估算目前評價為 PER 26X，考量過往
本益比區間為 25X~100X，目前評價處於低緣，主要反映資本市場下行，
拖累民眾消費動能縮減所致，考量 2027 年營收仍將持續創高並維持雙
位數增長，並參考電子支付業務用戶擴張至 450 萬人，有望加速帶動電
子支付業務增長，毛利率外溢效果亦將於 2027 年逐步顯現，再加上韓
國、日本等海外市場深化跨境支付，進一步提升 LinePay 獲利能力，因
而投研部調升至買進投資評等，目標價 303 元(2027F PE 30X)。

7722 LINEPAY 加權指數

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

拓展海外市場提升用戶數量及支付據點，公司目標 2026 年交易量突破 1 兆元 
2025 年財務分析中，營收主要來自於手續費收入佔 82%，而金融推廣服務亦佔約 14%，
全年毛利率則為 32.7%，若拆分為細項的話，作為營收主體的手續費收入毛利率約為
20%，金融推廣服務則有 93%。而截至 4Q25，身為國內第三方支付龍頭的 LinePay
用戶數已超過 1,360 萬人、平均每 2 人就有 1 人使用，而用戶綁卡數亦超過 2,100 萬
張，佔全體流通信用卡數逾 1/3，全台支付據點也已成長至 66 萬間，韓國支付據點亦
逾 8 萬間，全球支付據點加總超過 74 萬間，2025 年全年 LinePay 交易額亦達 8,680
億元。 
圖 1：2025 年 LinePay 營收收入及佔比

資料來源：LinePay 
穩定幣逐漸成全球趨勢、台灣政府若開放，LinePay 可望受惠 
隨美國天才法案通過，2025 年全球掀起穩定幣浪潮，美國、香港、新加坡、日本及英
國相繼提升其國內使用穩定幣比例，而穩定幣主要用途有兩項，其一為跨境支付，通過
以穩定幣跨境支付，相較於過往經由銀行結算系統進行跨境支付時，將可節省大量的
手續費成本以及交易時間，因此其為目前主要使用用途，而第二項用途則是提供避險
手段，在阿根廷及其餘新興國家中，其本國貨幣通膨較為嚴重，民眾普遍不信任政府發
行之法幣，而穩定幣則在其中提供穩定的美元避險功能，因此穩定幣亦於新興國家中
大幅使用。而考量 LinePay 具有足夠科技實力建立穩定幣平台並發行之，因而雖台灣
監管力道趨嚴，但台灣政府若開放，預期 LinePay 可望受惠而持續具成長空間。 
圖 2：穩定幣近年來成長幅度

資料來源：The Block

圖 3：各國穩定幣流通量百分比

資料來源：Artemis 
2026 年前 9 月營收維持雙位數增長，惟毛利率因營業費用轉列營業成本而縮減 
LinePay 2026 年累計前 9 月營收為 64.33 億元，YoY+15%，而 LinePay 前 9 月毛利
率為 24.9%，YoY-8.6ppts.，主因電子支付上線後，原本列為營業費用的管理與研發支
出，部分轉列至營業成本所致，惟毛利率亦較 1H26 有所提升，反映電子支付營收缺
口逐漸補上，此外由於 LinePay 2025 年申請電支執照及持續投入 IDC，使其研發費用
持續維持高點，以及行銷及管理費用亦有所增長，預期在電支平台建置完成及部分營
業費用轉列至營業成本後，整體營業費用率下降至 18%，YoY-6.8ppts.，惟營業淨利
率仍受成本及費用增長而降至 6.8%，YoY-1.9ppts.，且 3Q26 稅後淨利受業外匯兌損
失影響下較 3Q25 YoY-22.6%下，令整體 2026 年前 9 月稅後淨利僅增至 4.35 億元，
YoY+5.7%，EPS 為 6.12 元。 
2026 年營收預估將因短期營收出現缺口使年增低於過往水準 
綜觀 4Q26，LinePay 10 月營收為 7.33 億元，YoY+4.1%，占 4Q26 達成率為 31.6%，
約略符合投研部預期，營收年增低於過去水準主要反映 2025 年 12 月起轉入自營電子
支付，結束部分合作合約，短期營收出現缺口所致，考量前 9 月核心手續費年增維持
逾 20%，再加上電子支付業務已於 4 月轉虧為盈，此外 4Q26 亦有百貨週年慶、電商
節慶與旅遊旺季可望為支付交易量帶來季節性動能，因而投研部預估其營收成長幅度
雖將較 2025 年收窄，惟 4Q26 營收及獲利有望改善，綜合考量之下，投研部預估 4Q26
營收為 23.17 億元，按季節成長率來看 QoQ+5%，YoY+6.4%，稅後淨利則為 1.56 億
元，QoQ+11.8%，YoY+63.3%，EPS 2.29 元。 
電支業務有望加速拓展，調升至買進評等，目標價 303 元 
展望 2026 年，考量公司與眾多銀行推出多檔活動，預期將可挹注全年交易量提升，並
帶動營收成長，並參考 LinePay 開始施行電子支付業務，向店家收取之手續費收入不
必再與 iPASS MONEY 拆分收益，預期亦將挹注營收，惟參考 2025 年 12 月起轉入自
營電子支付，結束部分合作合約，短期營收出現缺口，所幸隨乘車碼、好友推薦、未成
年帳戶等多元場景擴大用戶規模，帶動電子支付用戶擴張至 450 萬人，並有望加速帶
動電子支付業務增長，綜合考量下，投研部預估 2026 年營收為 87.5 億元，YoY+11.2%，
此外，因電子支付業務毛利率較現行手續費毛利率高，預估隨電子支付業務 4 月轉虧
為盈後，將有望帶動毛利率逐季改善，因而預估將使毛利率較 2026 年前 9 月增長至
25.2%，YoY-7.5ppts.，且 LinePay 與眾多銀行皆有合作關係，在推出電子錢包服務
後，亦可與部分不願意使用信用卡之店家進行不同模式的合作，而隨著電支業務上線，

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
預期研發費用將有所縮減，再加上電子支付部分，銀行將負擔大部分行銷費用，綜合考
量下也將節省部分營業費用，預期亦能使淨利有所增長，稅後淨利則為 5.91 億元，
YoY+16.5%，EPS 為 8.58 元。 
2027 年部分，則預期在電子支付業務成長穩定，再加上海外獲利增長而使整體營收及
稅後淨利再度增長，投研部預估 LinePay 2027 年營收為 103.54 億元，YoY+18.3%，
稅後淨利則為 6.86 億元，YoY+16%，EPS 10.09 元。 
綜合前述，以 11/20 收盤價 225 元估算目前評價約為 PER 26X，考量 LinePay 過往
本益比區間為 25X~100X，目前評價處於低緣，主要反映資本市場下行，拖累民眾消
費動能縮減所致，考量 LinePay 2027 年營收仍將持續創高並維持雙位數增長，且非
現金支付仍有成長空間，再加上電子支付業務挹注後，整體成長潛力仍具前景，並考
量 Line pay money 電子支付公司運用乘車碼、好友推薦、未成年帳戶等多元活動擴
大用戶規模，帶動電子支付用戶擴張至 450 萬人，並有望加速帶動電子支付業務增
長，毛利率提升之外溢效果亦將於 2027 年逐步顯現，再加上韓國、日本等海外市場
亦透過高額回饋與點數活動深化跨境支付，進一步提升 LinePay 獲利能力，因而投研
部調升至買進投資評等，目標價 303 元(2027F PE 30X)。

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
90 
SK하이닉스  (000660) 
2026.11.04

HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

업황 우려에 고유 할인까지 겹친 과도한 하락 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 230만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다[표1]. 이는 상장 이후 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 해당한다. 
‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 102%, 66%, 47%로, ‘26년 정점 이후에도 과거 업사이클 
고점보다 높은 수준이 유지될 전망이다.  
한편, 이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해져 시장의 시야가 ‘28년으로 향할 경우 
‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 360만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 57% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -51%를 넘어섰고, 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
그러나 당사는 아래와 같은 투자포인트와 향후 주가 흐름을 제시한다.

‘28년 HBM 가치, 시가총액의 1.3배 
HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 cHBM으로 
갈수록 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 
물량과 가격이 결정된다. 동사는 이 전환이 업계에서 가장 선두권이다.  
당사는 동사 HBM 매출이 ‘26년 43조원에서 ‘28년 183조원으로 증가할 것으로 
전망한다. 만약, 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E(21배, 
15배, 12배)를 적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 47%, 93%, 135%를 
설명한다[표3]. ‘27년에 이미 HBM 하나만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명된다는 
의미다. 시장이 통상적으로 받아들이는 멀티플 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 
다르지 않다.

150만원에 사면 ‘30년 EPS 2배 
동사의 주주환원은 자사주 매입/소각 중심일 것으로 상정한다. 당사는 동사가 현 
정책 기간(‘25~’27년)에 이어 ‘28년 이후에도 FCF의 50%를 환원하고 이 중 
70%를 자사주 매입/소각에 쓸 것으로 가정했다.  
당사 가정대로라면 총주주환원수익률은 현 시가총액 대비 ‘27년 14%, ‘28~’30년 
연 17~24%다[표4]. 
관건은 매입 단가다. 자사주 매입의 주당가치 개선 효과는 P/E가 낮을수록 
커지는데, 현 주가의 ‘27년 P/E는 2.7배다. 현 주가 수준인 150만원에 매년 균등 
매입한다고 ‘30년까지 주식수는 50% 줄어든다.   
그 결과 ‘30년 주당순이익은 매입이 없을 때 대비 2배가 된다[표4]. ‘28년 이후 
이익이 정체되더라도 주당 이익은 계속 늘어나는 구조이며, 이익 지속성에 대한 
의심이 주가를 누를수록 소각 효과는 커진다. 현 주가 수준의 정체가 장기화되기 
어려운 이유다.

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간이다. 솔리다임 상장 철회에 이어 3분기 
실적발표에서 ‘26년 주주환원 100조원과 매 분기 자사주 매입 방침이 제시되면서 환원 
의지는 검증되었다. 
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 불확실성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 가격 하락폭은 LTA 
방어력이 확인되기 전까지 블랙박스다. 이 불확실성이 멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 
계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 구간이 이어질 전망이다. 이 구간의 
기회비용은 환원으로 보상받는다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 신규 팹 물량이 소량 
출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 당사는 2H27에 ‘28년 HBM 협의와 
LTA 연장이 지속적으로 확인되면서 이익이 스트레스 테스트 범위 안에서 유지될 것으로 
전망하며[그림2], 이것이 숫자로 확인되는 시점에 순환 할인이 축소되고 재평가가 나타날 
것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 2,000,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.21) 
1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

산업 투자의견 
- Overweight(비중확대) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 초과 상승 예상 
- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상 
- Underweight(비중축소) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률 대비 하회 예상

기업 투자의견 
- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 -10%p~10%p 주가 변동 예상 
- Underperform(시장수익률 하회) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 하락 예상

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

(Dưới 3 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15)

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.
Investment Implications
Oil market conditions remain a swing
factor for the outlook. A shift lower in
oil risk premia and improvement on
the refining front would go a long way

toward easing inflation and interest
rates while bolstering risk-asset
appetite. A gradual increase in Fixed
Income portfolio duration continues to
make sense, in our view.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings have generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than two-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.”2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market.2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of investment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractive in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market moves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting government
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff
Source: Chief Investment Office. Data as of October 22, 2026.
* “Pop the Champagne,” Portfolio Insights, Chief Investment Office, August 2026.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.

oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: risks mount
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
10:31 November 20 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
Australia’s labour market disappointed again in October, employment falling 43.5k following
September’s 36.4k decline. On a three-month average basis, job gains over the year have now slowed
to 1.1%yr, materially below current growth in the working-age population, 1.7%yr. As a result, the

unemployment rate rose to a new post-pandemic high of 4.9% despite the participation rate falling
another 0.2ppts to 66.6%.

### Page 2

While employment growth’s descent initially reflected softer momentum in hiring, now we are seeing
evidence of a material increase in redundancies. Most affected workers look to have remained in
employment to date, but persistent job shedding raises the risk of a faster and more persistent uptrend
in the unemployment rate, potentially above our baseline peak forecast of 5.2% (at mid-2027) and most
certainly before the late-2028 peak of 5.1% the RBA expects.

As such, the robust 1.1% (3.5%yr) rise in the Q3 Wage Price Index warrants close scrutiny. The Q3
increase was skewed towards those on awards and enterprise arrangements – pay outcomes directly
tied to the Fair Work Commission’s decision and collective bargaining. Individual arrangements
meanwhile, the most responsive to current and expected labour market conditions, accounted for their
smallest share of a Q3 wage increase since 2019. Business survey detail also suggests labour cost

growth will normalise quickly after the award increase.
Deteriorating economic conditions are also increasingly evident in housing. The Westpac Housing
Sentiment Index fell 2.2% to 83.2 in November, among the weakest readings since 2005. Interestingly,
there remains considerable latent demand: almost 13% of consumers intend to purchase a home over
the next year, and relatively few plan to sell. For now, though, weak sentiment, high interest rates and

expectations of further price declines are keeping would-be buyers on the sidelines. These factors also
pose risks to the broader growth story.
In the US, abstracting from the Anthropic IPO, the outlook for inflation remained the critical theme. The
October FOMC minutes highlighted that inflation is currently being supported by tariffs and energy
prices as well as the AI buildout, with "several [members] judg[ing] that price pressures were
increasingly extending beyond industries directly affected by data center construction”. Participants

"assessed that medium- and longer-term inflation expectations continued to be consistent with the
Committee's 2 percent objective", although persistent above-target inflation is a threat to the status
quo, and perceived risks are skewed to the upside. FOMC members who spoke throughout the week
also kept the focus on price risks, and called for the FOMC to act swiftly. Governor Waller again
proposed a 50bp hike in December; others seemingly favour a 25bp hike at the December and January

meetings, as Westpac forecasts, and continuing to tighten further in 2027 if necessary.
Very clearly, the labour market and activity growth are not of concern for US policy makers. Retail sales
gave further justification for this view this week, the control group surprising to the upside with a 0.6%
gain in October after a material upward revision to September (0.5%). That said, housing is under
considerable pressure, pending home sales falling 17.1% over the past year, and housing starts back

at 2019 levels despite a 9.9% rebound in October. Persistent weakness in this sector will, in time, have
implications for both consumption and investment.
China’s October data round was the other key international development of the week. Again, it provided
a call for pro-active stimulus in scale. In the month, annual retail sales growth almost stalled at 0.4%yr
and the decline in fixed asset investment broadened outside property, with capital expenditure now

down 8.1% across the economy year-to-date. Industrial production again highlighted that its output to
satiate global demand which is holding up aggregate growth; though, even here, momentum softened
in October, annual growth in production slowing from 5.5%yr to 4.8%yr. It is not only activity that

### Page 3

Related articles
remains under pressure, but also wealth, with new / existing home prices 0.15% / 0.24% lower in the
month. As we continue to emphasise, to prove effective, stimulus needs to affect change in both activity
and wealth and do so in a sustainable manner.

Document 385: OPEC+：拟维持现有产量配额至1月，本周日将商讨2027年产量目标

Source PDF: all report pdf/OPEC+：拟维持现有产量配额至1月，本周日将商讨2027年产量目标.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

OPEC+正考虑将现⾏⽯油产量配额延续⾄2027年1⽉，以应对中东冲突及市场不确定性。
由于海湾原油出⼝受限，名义配额上调已难以转化为实际供应。与此同时，伊拉克质疑产
能评估结果，阿联酋退出也加剧了联盟内部协调难度，2027年产量⽬标仍存变数。
摘要：
OPEC+正考虑暂缓调整⽯油产量政策，将现⾏配额延续⾄明年1⽉。在中东冲突扰乱海湾原油
供应、成员国产能评估迟迟未能落地以及联盟内部⽭盾加深的背景下，维持现状成为各⽅应对
不确定性的折中选择。
11⽉26⽇，据彭博援引两名知情代表，OPEC+正考虑将现⾏⽯油产量配额延续⾄2027年1⽉，
以应对中东冲突及其他市场不确定性。这是联盟正在讨论的选项之⼀。OPEC+定于本周⽇举⾏
线上会议，讨论2027年的新产量⽬标。会议召开之际，成员国既需要为冲突可能结束后的供应
恢复做准备，也必须处理联盟内部⽇益明显的利益分歧。
不过，延续配额对实际供需的影响可能⼗分有限。Energy Aspects创始⼈兼董事Amrita Sen在
采访中表⽰，从供需平衡⻆度看，延续配额不会带来实质差别。⽬前，各成员国基本按照⾃⾝

⽣产能⼒安排产量，但海湾地区原油出⼝仍受到多重限制。
名义配额与实际供应脱节
过去⼀年半，OPEC+部分成员国通过⼀系列配额上调，逐步恢复了2023年暂停的两批产量。即
便中东冲突导致波斯湾部分国家关闭产能、原油供应受到⼲扰，联盟仍在继续推进增产计划，
使得名义产量⽬标与实际供应之间的差距进⼀步扩⼤。
这也意味着，延续配额并不等于冻结实际供应。在出⼝能⼒受限的情况下，即使成员国获得更
⾼的⽣产配额，也未必能够将新增原油运往市场。当前油市⾯临的核⼼约束已不只是⽣产能
⼒，还包括原油能否顺利出⼝。因此，名义配额的调整对短期实际供应的影响相对有限。
⻓期产量安排同样⾯临障碍。OPEC+原计划将外部产能审查结果作为2027年配额分配的依据，
但这⼀进程因伊拉克对评估结果提出异议⽽变得复杂。
11⽉16⽇，OPEC代表为这项产能评估举⾏了“闭幕会议”，并将其称为“历史性⾥程碑”。评估由
总部位于达拉斯的咨询机构DeGolyer & MacNaughton执⾏，具体结果⼀直在联盟内部严格保
密。由于评估结果直接关系到各成员国未来的配额分配，迟迟⽆法形成⼀致意⻅，也使2027年

产量⽬标缺乏明确的核定基础。
成员国分歧加剧，2027年⽬标仍存变数
产能评估受阻的背后，是OPEC+内部⽇益复杂的利益关系。阿联酋在多年不满产量限制后于5
⽉退出联盟，伊拉克则持续争取更⼤的增产空间。成员国数量变化与各⽅⽣产诉求分化，令联
盟协调产量政策的难度进⼀步上升。
俄罗斯的表态也显⽰出当前决策的谨慎。作为OPEC+第⼆⼤产油国，俄罗斯副总理Alexander
Novak周三表⽰，联盟将在年底前审议产能审计结果，并将在周⽇会议上了解相关⼯作的进展。
据塔斯社报道，Novak并未透露具体的产量政策安排。

Document 438: When tokenization meets securitized debt markets (2026-11-17)

### Page 2

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
When tokenization meets securitized debt markets
Asset tokenization continues to grow in 2026. Excluding stablecoins, the
market grew from ~$10bn in Jan 2025 to ~$40bn in Nov 2026. Including
tokens confined to closed or permissioned environments, the total reached
~$420bn (here).
This chartbook explores tokenization’s potential in securitized markets, where

complex structuring, long settlement times and high transaction costs make
the asset class a strong candidate for tokenization.
Today, tokenization mainly improves securities-market infrastructure by
streamlining intermediation, cutting loan origination costs and speeding up
institutional securitization. US-based companies Figure Technologies,
Centrifuge and Maple Finance illustrate the benefits.
The transformative end state combines tokenized infrastructure (represented

tokenization) with tokens transferable outside the issuing platform (distributed
tokenization). Only when both are combined could tokenization reduce credit
risk, deepen the investor base, speed up the flow of capital, and lead to a more
robust, liquid US securitized market.
In this chartbook, we: (i) explain securitization and its challenges; (ii) examine
tokenization’s potential benefits; and (iii) outline the steps needed for
widespread adoption.

….but only ~$8bn in distributed value.
Sources: Deutsche Bank Research, RWA.xyz. Data as of Nov 12, 2026. Credit combines asset-backed credit,
corporate credit, diversified credit and specialty finance. “Represented” refers to tokens using the blockchain
as a recordkeeping layer, enhancing transparency, reconciliation, and operational efficiency without enabling
onchain investor transfer or distribution.
Sources: Deutsche Bank Research, RWA.xyz. Data as of Nov 13, 2026. “Distributed”

refers to tokens transferable outside the issuing platform and between wallets; it does
not measure trading activity.
Tokenized credit’s total value is ~$50bn in November, with Figure’s HELOC
token representing $26trn…

### Page 3

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
A ~$15trn market financing housing, consumers and companies
❑ The securitized (structured credit) market includes residential real estate
(RMBS), commercial real estate (CMBS), consumer borrowing (ABS –
autos, credit cards, student loans), and corporate debt (CLOs).
❑ It is a material part of the US growth engine: ~$15trn of debt outstanding,
roughly half of 2025 US nominal GDP. About 75% is linked to US housing

market.
❑ This market emerged in the late 1970s, when the US economy struggled
with sluggish growth and high inflation from elevated energy costs.
❑ Securitization was seen as financial innovation: It let bank lenders move
debt off their balance sheets and syndicate it to public markets as a
pooled asset.
❑ In the three decades before the GFC, the market’s growth increased
capital velocity in US debt markets while simultaneously reducing
financing costs for both borrowers and originators.

❑ Securitization produces pricing efficiency by bundling a diversified pool
of assets with consistent cashflows (e.g., such as corporate loans or
monthly mortgage payments) into a security held by a bankruptcy remote
special purpose vehicle (SPV).
❑ These pooled assets are then sliced into “tranches” with varying credit
risk and corresponding levels of yield, sold to institutional investors (life
insurers to hedge funds) that find relative value in the structure and

diversification.
Residential mortgages (RMBS) plays an outsized role in the market
The US Securitized market is approaching $16trn in market size
Sources: SIFMA, Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research.
Sources: SIFMA, US Federal Reserve, Deutsche Bank Research.

### Page 4

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
Securitization is held back by complexity, slow settlement and high costs
A CLO investor can expect their first coupon payment typically 6 months
after a deal’s pricing date
Although securitization is deeply embedded into the US financial
system, several challenges remain:
❑ Inherent complexity: Collateralization complicates ownership
tracking, especially as deals are upsized, widely distributed and

traded in a secondary market. This was visible during the GFC, and in
recent pockets of credit stress – the tracking of collateral and of
asset ownership can present logistical hurdles over the lifetime of an
asset.
❑ Long settlement times: Complex structuring can delay settlement
and prolong the liquidity gap between allocating capital to a deal and
when the first coupon distribution takes place.  For example, a new
issue CLO can take up to two months from pricing to close, with an

additional four months before the first coupon distribution date takes
place.
❑ High transaction costs: Intermediaries between the buyer and seller
provide essential services but increase costs when issuing a deal.
Additionally, the ongoing deal administration from monthly collateral
monitoring to coupon distributions adds an additional layer of
logistical complexity to a securitization post close. The key
stakeholders required in this value chain range from arranging banks,

trustees, rating agencies, and lawyers.
Source: Bloomberg Finance LP, Deutsche Bank Research.
4

### Page 5

Marion Laboure, Camilla Siazon, Jamie Flannick | Tokenized Securitization |  November 2026
❑ Tokenization records assets or claims as digital tokens on a distributed
ledger (i.e., blockchain), using public or permissioned networks.
❑ Potential benefits: Faster transfers, automated servicing and smaller
investment units.
❑ Shared records can reduce reconciliation, but trading hours, redemption
terms and investor access remain product-specific.
Two issuance models:

❑ On-chain (native): The security or fund share is issued directly on the
distributed ledger/blockchain.
❑ Off-chain (tokenized representation): The underlying asset remains
outside the blockchain, but ownership claims and economic rights are
represented digitally on-chain.
❑ Tokens may be then either transferred to external wallets (distributed) or
platform-bound on the blockchain (represented).
Tokenization can move issuance, settlement and servicing onto a shared ledger
